### [1] [Gradient-Based Latent Decomposition Reveals Mechanisms of Feature Degradation in Weakly Supervised Mammography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24835)
*Vinceline Bertrand,Ionut Cardei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于梯度的正交潜在分解方法，用于解释分层变分自编码器（H-VAEs）在乳腺癌筛查中存在的“粗粒度特征保留而细粒度恶性线索退化”的不对称现象。研究发现仅约4.4%的潜在变量与监督信号对齐，其余95.6%位于正交残差空间，细粒度病理预测主要依赖此空间，导致重建稳定性与分类性能之间存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱监督分层模型在乳腺癌筛查中存在持续性不对称问题：粗粒度病变类型特征在重构中得到保留，而细粒度的恶性指征却发生退化，这直接影响了临床诊断流程的可靠性。旨在通过机制性解释这一现象，提升模型的可解释性与临床实用性。

Method: 引入基于梯度的正交潜在分解技术应用于分层变分自编码器（H-VAEs）。将潜在空间划分为两部分：由粗粒度监督梯度塑造的任务对齐组件（$z_1$）和捕获剩余表征能力的正交残差（$z_{\text{res}}$）。使用CBIS-DDSM数据库中的3,550个乳腺X线影像感兴趣区域（ROI）进行训练与评估，并与多实例学习（MIL）和多任务学习（MTL）进行对比。

Result: 1. 仅约4.4%的潜在幅度与监督梯度对齐，约95.6%位于正交残差中，细粒度病理预测主要依赖该残差空间。
2. 模型达到Stage-1 AUC 0.866和Stage 2 AUC 0.552。
3. 存在显著的重建稳定性差距（$\Delta_{\text{diag}}=5\%, p=0.005$）和分类差距（$\Delta_{\text{AUC}}=0.314, p<0.001$）。
4. 潜在消融实验证实两类特征均重度依赖于$z_{\text{res}}$，结构性地解释了为何重构会导致病理稳定性不成比例地下降。
5. 跨架构和模态的比较验证了方法的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 在高维空间中，单一的粗粒度监督信号仅能隔离出一个稀疏的一维潜在方向，迫使关键的细粒度特征进入脆弱的残差子空间。这种结构性限制是导致细粒度特征在重构过程中退化的根本原因，揭示了高维表示学习中监督信号分布对模型性能的关键影响。

Abstract: Weakly supervised hierarchical models exhibit a persistent asymmetry: coarse lesion-type features are preserved under reconstruction while fine-grained malignancy cues degrade---a pattern with direct consequences for the clinical reliability of breast cancer screening pipelines. We introduce gradient-based orthogonal latent decomposition for hierarchical Variational Autoencoders~(H-VAEs) to mechanistically explain this asymmetry. The latent space is partitioned into a task-aligned component~($z_1$), shaped by coarse supervisory gradients, and an orthogonal residual~($z_{\text{res}}$) capturing remaining representational capacity. On~3,550 mammographic Regions of Interest~(ROIs) from CBIS-DDSM, only~$\sim$4.4\% of latent magnitude aligns with supervisory gradients, leaving~$\sim$95.6\% in the orthogonal residual upon which fine-grained pathology prediction primarily depends. The model achieves Stage-1~AUC~0.866 and Stage 2~AUC~0.552, with a reconstruction stability gap of $Δ_{\text{diag}}=5\%$ ($p=0.005$) and a classification gap of $Δ_{\text{AUC}}=0.314$ ($p{<}0.001$). Latent ablation confirms that features for both tasks reside heavily in~$z_{\text{res}}$, structurally explaining why reconstruction degrades pathology stability disproportionately. Comparisons with Multi-Instance Learning~(MIL) and Multi-Task Learning~(MTL) confirm generalization across architectures and modalities. These findings reveal that in high-dimensional spaces, a single coarse supervisory signal isolates only a sparse 1D latent direction, forcing critical fine-grained features into the vulnerable residual subspace.

</details>


### [2] [Track-Leakage-Free Hold-Out Self-Validation for Photogrammetric Reconstruction: Protocol, Sensitivity, and Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24852)
*Behnam Asadi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究评估了一种“无轨迹泄漏”的保留协议，用于通过3D重建的内部自洽性来估计其可靠性。结果表明，虽然该协议能有效检测内部几何一致性并识别导致结构破碎的重大故障，但它无法准确反映绝对精度（如RTK误差），也无法作为通用重大故障筛选器，因为全局扭曲但内部一致的重建仍可获得高置信度分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化摄影测量检查通常从3D重建中发出度量测量值，但这些重建本身的正确性在没有外部调查的情况下通常是未知的。本研究旨在探讨是否可以在没有地面真值的情况下，让重建自我估计其可靠性。

Method: 作者形式化了一种“无轨迹泄漏”的保留协议：确定性地隐藏一部分图像，并将这些被隐藏的图像仅与至少由两个保留图像看到的3D点进行重新定位。这种方法建立了一个“轨迹级屏障”，确保视图永远不会针对其帮助创建的结构进行测试。所有观察结果（包括被隐藏的）都进入构建可信结构的束调整（bundle adjustment）。最终聚合为一个mAA（mean Average Accuracy）置信度指标。该方法在GNSS参考捕获以及ETH3D、EuRoC和IMC 2025基准测试上进行了验证。

Result: 1. **计算合理性**：良好的重建表现出毫弧度级的自洽性得分。
2. **置信度与精度脱节**：阈值化的自洽性会饱和，且不追踪绝对精度。即使真实RTK误差在一个捕获内波动高达14倍，置信度仍接近1.00。跨五个捕获的相关性符号不稳定（-0.57至+0.98）， pooled 95% CI跨越零，表明其不能用于门控准确性。
3. **仅标记严重故障**：只有当故障破坏内部一致性时（如模型破碎），置信度才会下降。然而，一个单一但全局扭曲且内部一致的重建模型可以逃避检测（四个捕获中有三个模型错误达55-106米，但置信度为1.00）。
4. **独立数据验证**：在独立的ETH3D和IMC 2025地面真值上发现了相同的二分法现象。

Conclusion: “无轨迹泄漏”保留协议衡量的是内部几何一致性，而非绝对精度。它既不能替代控制点评估，也不能作为通用的重大故障筛选器。作者发布了该协议及退化模拟工具。

Abstract: Automated photogrammetric inspection emits metric measurements from a 3D reconstruction whose own correctness is normally unknown without an external survey. Can a reconstruction estimate its own reliability with no ground truth? We formalise a track-leakage-free hold-out protocol: a deterministic subset of images is withheld and each re-localised against only 3D points seen by at least two retained images -- a track-level barrier so a view is never tested against structure it helped create -- aggregated into an mAA confidence. Held-out observations still entered the bundle adjustment that built the trusted structure, so we call it track-leakage-free (a stricter re-mapping variant confirms this at 3/5 deg). Across operational GNSS-referenced captures plus ETH3D, EuRoC and the IMC 2025 benchmark we report four findings. (i) The protocol is computationally well-posed: good reconstructions score millidegree self-consistency. (ii) Thresholded self-consistency saturates and does not track absolute accuracy: confidence stays near 1.00 while true RTK error swings up to 14x within a capture, and the per-capture correlation is sign-unstable across five captures (-0.57 to +0.98; pooled 95% CI spanning zero), so it cannot gate accuracy. (iii) It flags gross failure only when the failure destroys internal consistency: a fragmenting model drops confidence, but a single self-consistent, globally-distorted model evades it -- three of four captures gave one model wrong by 55-106 m at confidence 1.00. (iv) The same dichotomy holds on independent ETH3D and IMC 2025 ground truth. Track-leakage-free hold-out measures internal geometric consistency, not absolute accuracy: neither a substitute for control-point assessment nor a general gross-failure gate. We release the protocol and degradation harness.

</details>


### [3] [DisasterTD: Disaster Toponym Disambiguation Using Multimodal LLMs and Cross-View Geolocalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24856)
*Wenping Yin,Ziqi Liu,Naixia Mou,Weijia Li,Danfeng Hong,Hao Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DisasterTD框架，结合多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的语义推理与跨视角地理定位技术，解决社交媒体图像中地名模糊导致的灾害地理定位难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体图像（SMI）能提供及时的灾情地面视角，但其包含的地名往往模糊不清，导致准确的地理定位困难。现有方法难以单独依靠文本或视觉信息实现高精度定位。

Method: 构建DisasterTD框架：1. 利用MLLM从噪声文本中提取地名并生成候选地理位置；2. 通过SMI、遥感图像（RSI）及可选街道视图图像（SVI）之间的跨视角匹配来验证和细化候选结果。在飓风哈维数据集上评估，按地名清晰度分为四类进行细粒度分析。

Result: DisasterTD在各项精度指标上均优于仅使用MLLM或仅使用跨视角匹配的基线模型。具体表现为：1000米内准确率达71.62%，50米内为47.01%；平均误差降至11.33公里，中位数误差为0.68公里。在地名模糊的场景下改进最为显著，有效减少了候选位置的分散性和误差。

Conclusion: 将基于MLLM的候选位置生成与跨视角验证相结合，能有效提升细粒度灾害地理定位的准确性，特别是在处理模糊地名时具有显著优势。

Abstract: Social media imagery (SMI) provides timely and fine-grained ground perspectives that are valuable for situational awareness and emergency response. Unlike satellite or aerial imagery, SMI can capture disaster impacts and ground-level conditions in a timely manner. However, geographic references in SMI are often vague or ambiguous, making accurate geolocalization challenging. To address this issue, we propose DisasterTD, a disaster toponym disambiguation framework that integrates multimodal large language model (MLLMs)-based semantic reasoning with cross-view geolocalization. First, MLLMs extract toponyms and generate candidate geolocations from noisy textual inputs. Then, cross-view matching between SMI, remote sensing imagery (RSI), and optionally street-view imagery (SVI) is used to verify and refine these candidate results. We evaluate DisasterTD on the Hurricane Harvey dataset, where SMI is augmented with collected RSI and SVI to construct a cross-view benchmark for disaster geolocalization. The dataset is divided into four categories based on toponym clarity and ambiguity, allowing a fine-grained performance analysis across scenarios. Results show that DisasterTD consistently outperforms MLLM-only and cross-view-only baselines without disambiguation, achieving geolocalization accuracies of 71.62% within 1000 m, 62.36% within 500 m, 57.99% within 250 m, 52.09% within 100 m, and 47.01% within 50 m, while reducing the mean and median errors to 11.33 km and 0.68 km, respectively. The largest improvements appear in ambiguous toponyms, where semantic reasoning with cross-view evidence reduces candidate dispersion and errors. These findings demonstrate the effectiveness of integrating MLLM-based candidate generation with cross-view verification for fine-grained disaster geolocalization.

</details>


### [4] [Harm is not Universal: Community-Specific Toxicity Detection is Urgently Needed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24898)
*Xinnuo Xu,Anja Thieme,Daniela Massiceti,Ioana Tanase,Rita Marques,Melanie Fernandez Pradier,Martin Grayson,Camilla Longden,Cecily Morrison*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出社区特异性毒性检测（CTD），指出通用模型对残障群体存在严重漏判，并通过实验证明专用指南和微调可提升检测效果，但整体性能仍远低于通用标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本生成图像（T2I）的毒性检测器采用“一刀切”模式，未能有效保护边缘化群体。实证数据显示约35%被标记为安全的图像在残障社区看来具有危害性，因此需要针对特定社区开发专用的毒性检测机制。

Method: 1. 与残障专家合作制定针对侏儒症和低视力/盲人群体的安全指南；2. 构建包含2,400个标注图像的T2I数据集；3. 评估零样本设置下大型视觉语言模型、通用检测器及提示工程方法（ICL, VQA）的性能；4. 测试参数高效微调（PEFT）在小模型上的表现。

Result: 通用检测器和大型模型在零样本下表现极差（F1分数低于随机猜测，分别为0.32和0.37）。提示适应方法显著提升了大模型性能（GPT-4o F1达0.50和0.78），小模型经少量数据微调后也有改善（最佳F1 0.48和0.59），但仍受限于指南变化。CTD性能远低于通用检测的F1≈0.9水平。

Conclusion: 社区特异性毒性检测在技术上可行且必要，但目前性能仍有巨大提升空间。鉴于其复杂性和对动态指南的敏感性，亟需持续的研究投入以缩小与通用检测性能的差距。

Abstract: State-of-the-art toxicity detectors for text-to-image generation adopt a one-size-fits-all approach: a single universal model applying fixed safety guidelines to all users. Our empirical evidence shows that these detectors fail to shield marginalized communities: approximately 35% of generated images labeled safe are considered harmful by disability communities. In this position paper, we argue for community-specific toxicity detection (CTD). To demonstrate its feasibility, we collaborate with disability experts to develop safety guidelines for two communities: dwarfism and blind/low vision. Using a dataset of 2,400 annotated T2I-generated images we demonstrate that both large vision-language models and existing general-purpose toxicity detectors catastrophically fail to recognize harmful content under these guidelines in zero-shot settings with F1 score lower than random guessing (F1 0.32 and 0.37). Promisingly, prompt-based adaptation methods (ICL, VQA) substantially improve harm detection performance (GPT-4o: F1 0.50 and 0.78), while parameter-efficient fine-tuning improves smaller models (0.5b-7b with best F1 0.48 and 0.59) with less than 100 demonstrations, but remains sensitive to evolving guidelines. Despite these gains, CTD performance remains far below F1 $\approx 0.9$ achieved for general-purpose toxicity detection, highlighting the challenge and the need for sustained research effort.

</details>


### [5] [Mage-VL: An Efficient Codec-Native Streaming Multimodal Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24904)
*Senqiao Yang,Kaichen Zhang,Zhaoyang Jia,Jinghao Guo,Yifei Shen,Xinjie Zhang,Xiaoyi Zhang,Haoqing Wang,Xiao Li,Peng Zhang,Xiang An,Yin Xie,Zhening Liu,Xun Guo,Jiahao Li,Shicheng Zheng,Jinglu Wang,Zongyu Guo,Wenxuan Xie,Zihan Zheng,Yuxuan Luo,Bin Li,Yan Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Mage-VL，一种高效的流式视觉语言基础模型。通过自定义编码器Mage-ViT选择性编码动态区域，大幅减少视觉Token消耗（>75%），结合双系统架构实现实时多模态交互，在保持静态任务性能的同时显著提升视频理解和推理速度（最高3.5倍加速）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决标准视觉语言模型（VLMs）面临的Moravec悖论：即擅长复杂离线视觉推理，但在简单流式感知任务上表现不佳且处理效率低下。旨在实现高效、实时的多模态理解与交互。

Method: 1. **Mage-ViT编码器**：基于16x16补丁级别，利用运动向量和残差能量，在稀疏参考帧(I)和预测帧(P)间选择性编码高熵动态区域，替代均匀帧采样。
2. **AI4AI数据流水线**：包含提示-代码联合优化的多模态描述生成及AI驱动的性能诊断，用于指导训练配方。
3. **双系统架构**：采用受生物启发的轻量级System 1事件门控和因果System 2解码器，实现主动流式感知。
4. **大规模预训练**：使用约5.6亿张无标签图像和1亿帧无标签视频帧从头训练。

Result: 1. **效率提升**：视觉Token消耗减少超过75%，推断速度最高提升3.5倍。
2. **性能对比**：Mage-VL-4B在静态任务上匹配Qwen3-VL-4B；在视频理解和2D/3D空间推理上显著优于Phi-4-reasoning-vision（15B基线）。
3. **编码器竞争力**：Mage-ViT在无标签数据上的表现媲美或超越数十亿图文对训练的旗舰编码器。
4. **实证发现**：总结了关于预训练数据效率、可变分辨率缩放、编解码系统加速等七项关键实证发现。

Conclusion: Mage-VL通过创新的编解码原生流式架构和数据管道，有效解决了VLM在流式感知中的效率瓶颈，实现了高性能与低延迟的统一，为实时多模态交互提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Standard vision-language models (VLMs) suffer from Moravec's paradox: they excel at complex offline visual reasoning but struggle with simple streaming perception tasks and process them inefficiently. We present Mage-VL, an efficient codec-native streaming foundation model for real-time multimodal understanding and interaction. At its core, our custom tokenizer, Mage-ViT, replaces uniform frame sampling by selectively encoding dynamic, entropy-rich regions using motion vectors and residual energy across sparse anchor (I) and predicted (P) frames. Operating at a 16 x 16 patch level, this reduces visual token consumption by over 75% while preserving spatiotemporal context. Trained from scratch on approximately 560M unlabeled images and 100M unlabeled video frames, Mage-ViT matches or outperforms flagship encoders trained on billions of image-text pairs. We establish AI4AI data pipelines encompassing prompt-code joint optimization for multimodal captioning and AI-driven performance diagnosis to guide training recipes. Furthermore, through a bio-inspired dual-system architecture - a lightweight System 1 event gate and a causal System 2 decoder - Mage-VL enables proactive streaming perception. Extensive evaluations show that Mage-VL-4B matches Qwen3-VL-4B on static tasks while achieving strong gains in video understanding and 2D/3D spatial reasoning, with up to a 3.5x wall-clock inference speedup, and comprehensively surpasses the 15B Phi-4-reasoning-vision baseline. Beyond model artifacts, we deliver seven key empirical findings covering pre-training data efficiency, variable-resolution scaling, codec system acceleration, VideoQA SFT redundancy, motion-spatial synergy, AI4AI data pipelines, and Zero-Vision SFT for multimodal RL.

</details>


### [6] [PerceptionBench: Evaluating Atomic Visual Perception in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24957)
*Zichao Lin,Yifeng Xie,Bowen Qu,Haiming Wang,Jia Li,Haoning Wu,Yuhao Dong,Zuhao Yang,Jinguo Zhu,Haoyu Lu,Zijia Zhao,Tongtian Yue,Zhangyang Qi,Junwei Yang,Mengfan Dong,Peizhou Cao,Chenzhuang Du,Zaida Zhou,Haotian Yao,Hao Yang,Hongcheng Gao,Lin Sui,Weihong Li,Xinxing Zu,Jia Chen,Yao Wang,Xiaoxue Wu,Yalin Wang,Y. Charles,Yiping Bao,Yangyang Liu,Zhiqi Huang,Xinyu Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PerceptionBench基准，通过自下而上的方法构建10种原子视觉感知能力，评估16个前沿多模态大语言模型（MLLMs），发现原子感知仍未解决，平均准确率低于60%，幻觉是最弱环节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试往往无法隔离纯粹的视觉感知能力：整体评估将感知错误与推理或领域知识失败混淆，而应用驱动型基准仅覆盖由启发式设计决定的狭窄、碎片化领域。

Method: 采用自下而上的方法，通过分析42个现有基准中前沿MLLMs的最早失败点，构建包含感知分支的错误分类法，定义10种原子感知能力；据此构建3,000道经过验证的问题，每道题仅隔离单一能力，难度源于感知而非推理或知识。

Result: 在16个前沿MLLMs上的基准测试显示：原子感知能力尚未得到充分解决，没有模型达到60%的准确率；感知相关幻觉是平均最弱的能力；总体得分相似掩盖了能力分布的巨大差异。

Conclusion: PerceptionBench为衡量和诊断MLLMs的视觉感知边界提供了一个基于能力层面的标准。

Abstract: We introduce PerceptionBench, a benchmark specifically designed to evaluate the atomic visual perception capabilities of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs). Existing benchmarks often fail to isolate perception: holistic evaluations conflate perceptual errors with failures in reasoning or domain knowledge, while application-driven benchmarks only cover narrow, fragmented domains shaped by heuristic designs. To address these limitations, PerceptionBench adopts a bottom-up approach: by diagnosing the earliest failure points in the responses of frontier MLLMs across 42 existing benchmarks, we construct an error taxonomy whose perception branch defines ten atomic perceptual capabilities. Guided by this taxonomy, we construct 3,000 verified questions with short, unambiguous answers, each isolating a single capability, with difficulty stemming from perception rather than reasoning or knowledge. Benchmark results across sixteen frontier MLLMs reveal that atomic perception remains largely unsolved---no model reaches 60\% accuracy, perception-related hallucination is the weakest capability on average, and similar overall scores conceal sharply divergent capability profiles. PerceptionBench thus provides a capability-level standard for measuring and diagnosing the visual perception boundaries of MLLMs.

</details>


### [7] [Enabling Fully Integer-Only Inference for Lightweight Detection Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24981)
*Thanh Cong Le,Michal Szczepanski,Martyna Poreba*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出I-LW-DETR，首个全整数轻量级DETR检测器，通过保留激活比例分裂卷积、符号依赖GELU近似和约束Shiftmax实现端到端整数运算，在显著降低模型体积（3.6倍）和计算成本（10倍以上）的同时保持适度精度损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量化检测器要么保留Softmax、GELu等非整数兼容算子，要么专注于重型骨干网络，缺乏针对轻量级检测Transformer的端到端整数实现，导致难以在NPU和微控制器上高效部署。

Method: 构建I-LW-DETR框架，包含三个核心组件：1) 尺度保留分裂卷积（为多尺度投影器的各分支分配独立激活尺度）；2) SD-ShiftGELu（基于符号的GELu近似，避免精度下降）；3) 约束Shiftmax（维持稳定的Softmax归一化）。所有前向传播操作均执行整数算术。

Result: 在不同模型规模下，该量化管道能持续生成高效的全整数模型；相比基准，模型大小减少约3.6倍，计算成本降低一个数量级以上，且精度下降幅度适中。

Conclusion: I-LW-DETR成功填补了轻量级检测Transformer全整数实现的空白，证明了在不严重牺牲精度的前提下，可实现高效的硬件友好型部署。

Abstract: Vision Transformer detectors now approach the accuracy of CNNs but remain difficult to deploy on NPUs and microcontrollers because key components, including deformable attention, feature fusion, and nonlinear activation functions, are not natively compatible with integer arithmetic. Existing quantized detectors either retain operators such as Softmax, GELU, and LayerNorm or focus on heavyweight backbones, leaving lightweight detection transformers without an end-to-end integer implementation. We address this gap with I-LW-DETR, the first fully integer-only lightweight DETR, in which every operation in the forward pass, including transformer nonlinearities, is executed in integer arithmetic. I-LW-DETR is built upon three key components: a scale-preserving split convolution that assigns independent activation scale to each branch of the multi-scale projector; SD-ShiftGELU, a sign-dependent GELU approximation that preserves element-wise behavior while avoiding the accuracy degradation; and a constrained Shiftmax that maintains stable Softmax normalization. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed quantization pipeline consistently produces efficient fully integer-only models across different model scales. Across all model scales, the proposed pipeline incurs only a moderate accuracy degradation while reducing the model size by approximately $3.6\times$ and the computational cost by more than one order of magnitude.

</details>


### [8] [Unifying Active Learning and Semi-Supervised Learning for Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25014)
*Bahram Jafrasteh,Cheng Wan,Heejong Kim,Johannes C. Paetzold,Qingyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RegAL框架，通过共享拓扑感知Pareto优化统一主动学习与半监督学习，解决冷启动阶段目标不匹配问题，在极端标注稀缺下显著提升医学图像分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际医疗场景中常面临超少标注数据（ultra-low labeled regimes）的挑战，现有主动学习（AL）和半监督学习（SSL）通常独立设计，导致早期“冷启动”阶段存在目标不匹配和训练不稳定问题。

Method: 提出RegAL统一框架，基于三个互补维度（体素不确定性、特征多样性、新型拓扑一致性指标）进行样本选择与 unlabeled 数据利用：1) 选择具有解剖信息边缘案例进行标注；2) 识别几何稳定图谱用于仿射配准增强，训练自监督Mean Teacher网络。

Result: 在BraTS 2021、dHCP和ProstateX数据集上，RegAL在极少标注卷情况下保持稳定，并在Dice系数及边界距离（ASD, HD95）指标上持续优于最先进的AL、SSL及主动半监督基线方法。

Conclusion: RegAL通过耦合样本获取与非标注数据利用，有效解决了极端标注稀缺下的训练稳定性与性能瓶颈，为医学图像分割提供了更高效的联合优化方案。

Abstract: In practical settings, medical image segmentation models are often developed with limited annotated data rather than fully labeled datasets. Training frequently begins in ultra-low labeled regimes where only a small number of volumes are annotated. In such scenarios, practitioners must simultaneously decide which cases to annotate and how to best use the remaining unlabeled data. Although active learning (AL) and semi-supervised learning (SSL) both target annotation scarcity, they are typically designed and optimized independently, resulting in objective mismatch and unstable training during early-stage "cold start" conditions. We propose RegAL, a unified active semi-supervised framework governed by a shared topology-aware Pareto optimization that couples sample acquisition with unlabeled data utilization. RegAL evaluates images along three complementary axes, voxel-wise uncertainty, feature diversity, and a novel topological consistency metric, to select anatomically informative edge cases for annotation. On the other hand, the same criteria are used to identify geometrically stable atlas candidates for diffeomorphic registration-guided augmentation to train a self-supervised Mean Teacher segmentation network. Across BraTS 2021, dHCP, and ProstateX, RegAL remains stable with few labeled volumes and consistently outperforms state-of-the-art AL, SSL, and active semi-supervised baselines across Dice and boundary-distance (ASD, HD95) metrics under extreme annotation scarcity.

</details>


### [9] [Diff-ID: Identity Consistent Facial Image Generation and Morphing via Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25078)
*Taimoor Rizwan,Sara Atito,Muhammad Awais,Zhenhua Feng,Josef Kittler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Diff-ID框架，通过双交叉注意力适配器和伪判别器损失，在保持高保真度的同时实现扩散模型中的身份一致性，并联合评估身份相似性与图像质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决高分辨率面部生成中身份保持的鲁棒性问题，这对于安全系统、生物识别认证等对身份完整性敏感的应用至关重要。

Method: 1. 构建21万张合成数据集（源自CelebA-HQ, FFHQ, LAION-Face）并使用微调BLIP进行标注以增强身份意识；2. 在微调的Stable Diffusion UNet中集成ArcFace和CLIP嵌入的双交叉注意力适配器；3. 提出基于ArcFace余弦相似度及指数时间步加权的伪判别器损失以强化身份保真度。

Result: 在未见面孔测试中，Diff-ID的原始ArcFace面部相似度略低于InstantID，但实现了显著更低的FID（Fréchet Inception Distance），并在评估方法中具有最强的FIQ（Face Image Quality）身份-真实性权衡表现；同时提供了无需逐身份微调的统一DDIM面部插值流水线。

Conclusion: 身份保持与照片级真实感应联合评估而非孤立看待，高身份相似度不保证输出真实感；建议将FIQ作为结合身份相似性与感知真实感的补充指标，与FS和FID共同作为主要评估标准。

Abstract: Generative diffusion models have revolutionized facial image synthesis, yet robust identity preservation in high resolution outputs remains a critical challenge. This issue is especially vital for security systems, biometric authentication, and privacy sensitive applications, where any drift in identity integrity can undermine trust and functionality. We introduce Diff-ID, a diffusion based framework that enforces identity consistency while delivering photorealistic quality. Central to our approach is a custom 210K image dataset synthesized from CelebA-HQ, FFHQ, and LAION-Face and captioned via a fine tuned BLIP model to bolster identity awareness during training. Diff-ID integrates ArcFace and CLIP embeddings through a dual cross attention adapter within a fine tuned Stable Diffusion UNet. To further reinforce identity fidelity, we propose a pseudo discriminator loss based on ArcFace cosine similarity with exponential timestep weighting. Experiments on held out and unseen faces show that Diff-ID does not exceed InstantID in raw ArcFace Face Similarity, but achieves substantially lower FID and the strongest FIQ based identity--realism trade off among the evaluated methods. We also present a unified DDIM based morphing pipeline that enables qualitative facial interpolation without per identity fine tuning. We further argue that identity preservation and photorealism should be evaluated jointly rather than in isolation, as high identity similarity alone does not guarantee realistic outputs. To make this trade off explicit, we report Face Image Quality (FIQ) as a complementary ratio based score that combines identity similarity and perceptual realism while keeping FS and FID as the primary metrics.

</details>


### [10] [MorphUNet: Alpha-Controlled Biometric Transport for Diffusion-Based Face Morphing Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25092)
*Taimoor Rizwan,Sara Atito,Zhenhua Feng,Muhammad Awais,Josef Kittler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MorphUNet，一种基于扩散模型的图像变形攻击框架。通过将双亲身份分解为CLIP外观和ArcFace身份证据，利用可训练的双交叉注意力机制在去噪U-Net中实现受alpha控制的生物特征传输。该方法在FEI和FRLL数据集上显著优于现有基线（如StableMorph, MIPGAN-II），实现了更高的变形攻击潜力（MAP）和更低的FID分数，且难以被检测系统识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对边境控制和身份验证系统中面临的合成图像威胁，特别是能够同时通过多个身份验证的“面部变形攻击”（Face morphing attacks）。现有的生成方法在保持双亲身份平衡性和生成图像质量方面存在不足，需要一种更高效、更难检测的合成方法。

Method: 1. **MorphUNet框架**：基于扩散模型，将两个父本的身份信息分解为CLIP外观特征和ArcFace身份证据。
2. **双交叉注意力机制**：首次在去噪U-Net中引入可训练的父本分离双交叉注意力层（Biometric Transport Layer），分别关注每个父本的身份证据，再通过变形参数alpha组合残差。
3. **优化策略**：使用DDIM反转潜在插值作为连贯的去噪起点；采用弱父本引导选择策略，优先保留相似度较低的父本特征，防止生成结果向单一贡献者坍塌。
4. **评估体系**：提出基于CFD（Conformal Fréchet Distance? 文中未明确全称，指代未见身份压力测试）的测试方法，涵盖性别/种族配对、人口统计学偏移及父本相似度极端情况。

Result: 1. **攻击成功率最高**：在FEI数据集上MAP达到0.919，FRLL数据集上为0.886（定义为至少欺骗六个识别系统中的三个）。
2. **图像质量最优**：在两个数据集上均获得最低的FID分数（FEI: 35.19, FRLL: 44.86）。
3. **抗检测能力强**：在同一数据集设置下，APCER@5%BPCER最高；在跨数据集迁移中仍极难检测（FEI APCER 0.996, FRLL APCER 0.946）。
4. **全面优势**：在Morphing Attack Potential (MAP), Morphing Attack Detection (MAD), 单系统脆弱性、身份平衡性、图像质量及鲁棒性测试中均表现最佳。

Conclusion: MorphUNet作为一种创新的扩散模型变形框架，通过精细的生物特征传输机制，成功生成了高保真且难以检测的变形图像。该研究揭示了当前身份验证系统在应对先进生成式攻击时的脆弱性，强调了开发更强鲁棒性检测算法的紧迫性。

Abstract: Face morphing attacks create synthetic images verifiable against multiple identities, threatening border control and identity verification systems. We introduce MorphUNet, a diffusion morphing framework formulating two-parent generation as alpha-controlled biometric transport: each parent is decomposed into CLIP appearance and ArcFace identity evidence, aligned into a CLIP-compatible token space, with the two contributors preserved as separate identity-aware token banks. To our knowledge, MorphUNet is the first diffusion-based morphing framework using trainable parent-separated dual cross-attention inside the denoising U-Net: a Biometric Transport Layer carrying parent-specific identity evidence through denoising, attending to each parent separately before combining residuals via the morphing parameter alpha. DDIM-inverted latent interpolation gives a coherent denoising start, while weaker-parent-guided selection favours morphs maximising the lower parent-similarity score, reducing collapse toward one contributor. We evaluate MorphUNet against three state-of-the-art baselines (StableMorph, MIPGAN-II, and MorDIFF) on FEI and FRLL using six recognition systems, and propose CFD-based unseen-identity stress testing across gender and ethnicity pairing, demographic shifts, and parent-similarity extremes. MorphUNet achieves the best Morphing Attack Potential (MAP) when at least three of six systems are fooled by one morph, reaching 0.919 on FEI and 0.886 on FRLL, and obtains the best FID on both datasets (35.19 FEI, 44.86 FRLL). It also gives the highest APCER at 5% BPCER in the same-dataset setting, and remains highly difficult to detect under cross-dataset transfer, with APCER 0.996 on FEI and 0.946 on FRLL. The full evaluation analyses MAP, MAD, per-system vulnerability, identity balance, image quality, top/bottom-similarity stress tests, and CFD unseen-identity robustness.

</details>


### [11] [IMPRINT: Image-Conditioned Query Enrichment for Long-Tail Object Goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25106)
*Jelin Raphael Akkara,Filippo Ziliotto,Luciano Serafini,Lamberto Ballan,Tommaso Campari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出IMPRINT框架，通过引入网络图像增强文本查询，解决细粒度物体导航中语义定位不准的问题，并在HSSD-rare新基准上验证了其零样本导航性能的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于预训练视觉-语言模型的零样本物体目标导航（ObjectNav）主要依赖纯文本查询，随着语义特异性增加至细粒度类别时，其可靠性显著下降。

Method: 构建IMPRINT框架：从网络获取与物体相关的图像，利用视觉-语言模型编码这些图像并与语义地图进行匹配以生成相似性图，最终聚合得到上下文感知的定位结果。该方法无需训练或修改底层导航策略。同时提出了HSSD-rare基准测试，专注于具有语义特异性子类别的长尾行为评估。

Result: 在OVON和HSSD-rare两个基准上，使用图像条件的查询均显著改善了物体定位精度并提升了端到端的导航性能。分析表明，定位增益转化为导航性能的关键在于下游检测质量，揭示了长尾具身导航中的系统瓶颈。

Conclusion: IMPRINT是一个即插即用的零样本框架，能有效通过多模态信息增强细粒度物体的定位能力；HSSD-rare基准为评估长尾导航提供了新标准，且后续研究需关注检测质量对整体导航性能的制约作用。

Abstract: Embodied AI increasingly relies on queryable semantic maps built from pre-trained vision-language models to enable zero-shot Object Goal Navigation (ObjectNav). However, existing approaches typically depend on text-only queries, which become less reliable as semantic specificity increases toward fine-grained object categories. We introduce IMPRINT, a zero-shot plug-and-play framework that enriches textual object queries with web-sourced images to improve grounding in queryable maps. Retrieved images are encoded using a vision-language model, matched against the semantic map to produce similarity maps, and aggregated to yield context-aware localization. Notably, this requires no training or modification of the underlying navigation policy. To explicitly evaluate long-tail behavior, we present HSSD-rare, a new ObjectNav benchmark built on Habitat Synthetic Scenes and featuring semantically specific subcategories. Across both OVON and HSSD-rare, image-conditioned queries consistently improve object grounding and yield end-to-end navigation gains. Further analysis reveals that translating localization gains to navigation performance depends critically on downstream detection quality, highlighting a key systems bottleneck in long-tail embodied navigation.

</details>


### [12] [OPERA: Offline Policy-guided Expert Routing and Adaptation for Universal Biomedical Image Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25108)
*Zihan Li,Feiyang Liu,Dandan Shan,Ruibo Wang,Qingqi Hong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出OPERA框架，通过离线策略学习专家路由和测试时适应，解决生物医学图像分析中的分布偏移问题，无需对专家模型进行梯度更新或重新训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物医学图像部署面临扫描仪、协议和患者群体带来的严重分布偏移，导致高性能模型需频繁进行领域特定微调，这在标签稀缺或隐私限制下成本高昂且不切实际。

Method: OPERA是一个多智能体集成框架，将专家权重分配视为离线策略学习问题：从少量验证集学习路由策略（无梯度更新），并结合测试时适应。核心机制包括：专家画像模块（离线选择策略）、温度缩放校准（提高概率可靠性）、分布感知适应（基于未标记测试数据动态调整类别权重）以及实例级路由（利用模型一致性和预测熵分配样本）。

Result: 在涵盖眼底摄影、胸部X光、CT、MRI及多模态诊断的9个数据集上，与30多个基线方法相比，OPERA在分类、分割和多模态设置中均持续提升了性能和校准质量。

Conclusion: 基于离线策略指导的专家代理协调是部署可操作生物医学AI的实用路径，无需重新训练即可有效应对分布偏移。

Abstract: Biomedical image analysis spans diverse modalities and tasks, yet real-world deployment is hindered by severe distribution shifts across scanners, protocols, and patient populations. High-performing models consequently require repeated domain-specific fine-tuning, which is a costly cycle that becomes impractical when labels are scarce or privacy constraints limit data sharing. We propose OPERA (Offline Policy-guided Expert Routing and Adaptation), a multi-agent ensemble framework that addresses this deployment bottleneck by treating expert weight assignment as an offline policy learning problem: a routing policy is learned from a small validation set without gradient updates to any expert agent, then deployed with test-time adaptation to handle distribution shift. OPERA coordinates heterogeneous specialist agents through complementary mechanisms. The expert profiling module learns selection policies offline, enabling informed allocation of expertise. Each agent undergoes confidence calibration through temperature adjustment, ensuring more reliable probabilistic outputs. OPERA also incorporates distribution aware adaptation, where class weights are dynamically adjusted at the batch level using statistics derived from unlabeled test data. Instance level routing assigns each sample to the most suitable expert by leveraging inter model agreement and predictive entropy. We evaluate OPERA on 9 datasets covering fundus photography, chest X-ray, CT, MRI, and multimodal diagnostic benchmarks, comparing against 30+ baselines across classification, segmentation, and multimodal settings. OPERA consistently improves performance and calibration quality, demonstrating that offline policy-guided expert agents coordination is a practical path to deployable biomedical AI without retraining. Code is on \href{https://github.com/HUANGLIZI/OPERA}{GitHub}.

</details>


### [13] [LENS: Adaptive Spatio-Temporal Zooming for Keyframe Sampling in Long-Form Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25125)
*Ce Zhang,Jinxi He,Katia Sycara,Yaqi Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LENS，一种无需训练的长视频关键帧采样框架，通过动态平衡空间细节（Zoom-in）和时间上下文（Zoom-out）来解决多模态大模型在长视频理解中面临的上下文窗口限制问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）进展迅速，但受限于上下文窗口大小，长视频理解仍是瓶颈。现有的关键帧采样方法难以在保留空间细节和覆盖时间范围之间取得平衡，导致时空搜索空间导航困难。

Method: 引入LENS框架，这是一种无需训练的关键帧采样方法。它根据文本查询动态决定何时进行“空间缩放”以获取细粒度细节，以及何时进行“时间缩放”以扩展时间范围。具体而言，LENS在有限的帧预算内自适应分配资源：空间缩放突出单帧中与查询相关的区域，时间缩放通过多帧聚合扩展时间范围，从而支持多粒度推理。

Result: 在多个长视频基准测试中，LENS consistently优于现有的最先进关键帧采样方法，并显著优于均匀采样。例如，在使用Qwen2.5-VL模型时，Video-MME准确率从53.3%提升至60.7%。

Conclusion: LENS通过自适应分配空间和时间注意力，有效解决了长视频理解中细节与上下文的冲突问题，显著提升了多模态模型在长视频任务上的性能。

Abstract: Despite rapid progress in Multi-modal Large Language Models (MLLMs), understanding long-form videos is still bottlenecked by limited context windows. While recent keyframe sampling methods attempt to mitigate this by distilling video inputs into a compact set of query-relevant frames, navigating the vast spatio-temporal search space remains challenging, as spatial detail and temporal coverage often conflict. To address this, we introduce LENS, a training-free keyframe sampling framework that dynamically decides when to zoom in for fine-grained details and when to zoom out for broader context based on the text query. Concretely, LENS adaptively allocates a limited frame budget between spatial zoom-ins, which highlight query-relevant regions within individual frames, and temporal zoom-outs, which expand the temporal scope through multi-frame aggregation, enabling the model to reason across multiple granularities while capturing both high-fidelity details and long-range context. Across diverse long-form video benchmarks, LENS consistently outperforms prior state-of-the-art keyframe sampling methods and delivers substantial gains over uniform sampling, improving Video-MME accuracy from 53.3% to 60.7% with Qwen2.5-VL.Code is available at https://github.com/zhangce01/LENS.

</details>


### [14] [FIDAC: An Easy-to-use Pipeline to Extract and Interpret Interpersonal Distance From Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25146)
*Keshav Rastogi,Eugy Han,Jeremy N. Bailenson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 开发了一个名为FIDAC的开源库，用于将面部检测结果转化为关于位置和人际距离的可操作数据，通过整合多个面部检测模型并优化追踪方法，以提高人际距离分析的准确性和实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然人与人之间距离能揭示相互感知的显著信息，但这类信息难以从视频输入中直接提取和解读。

Method: 开发了FIDAC库，合并多个开源面部检测模型的数据以弥补单一模型的不足；包含人工编码选择面部的流程以及减少深度失真的基准测试工具，以实现更准确的追踪。

Result: 成功构建了一个能够转化面部检测结果为位置和人际距离数据的工具，并通过多模型整合和特定追踪方法提升了数据准确性。

Conclusion: 未来计划评估FIDAC在不同深度和方向下测量人际距离的有效性，并进一步整合同步性等近景分析特征到软件中。

Abstract: The distance between persons reveals significant information about their perception of each other. However, such information is not easily extractable and interpretable from video input. We developed an open-sourced library, Facial Interpersonal Distance Analysis and Coding (FIDAC) that transforms facial detection results into actionable data about location and interpersonal distance. This tool merges data from multiple open-source facial detection models, strategically compensating for gaps in any individual model. In addition, we include methods for more accurate tracking, such as a pipeline for human coding of the selection of faces and a benchmarking tool to reduce depth distortion. For next steps, we plan on building upon FIDAC by evaluating its effectiveness at measuring interpersonal distance at various depths and orientations while further integrating features of proxemic analysis such as synchrony into its software.

</details>


### [15] [OpenPVMapper: A Multi-source, Nationwide Database of Rooftop Photovoltaic Systems in France](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25153)
*Gabiel Kasmi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出OpenPVMapper，这是法国大陆首个全面的屋顶光伏安装级数据库，通过整合深度学习遥感、OpenStreetMap和概率检测数据构建，包含113万余个安装点，精度约74-75%，开源发布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决目前缺乏公开、全面的光伏安装级别数据集的问题；现有公共注册表仅聚合高于容量阈值的数据，而基于遥感的检测研究通常局限于单一方法、有限地理范围或单一时间点。

Method: 构建一个全国性的多源数据库（OpenPVMapper），通过聚合和协调互补数据源：部署在全国航空影像上的基于深度学习的检测管道、OpenStreetMap数据以及概率性建筑级检测数据集。对数据进行去重、整合，并记录每个安装的来源、检测方法和人工验证标记。

Result: 生成的数据库包含1,135,850个光伏安装实例，总装机容量约15.01 GWp。通过对1,862个分层抽样样本进行人工审查，整体精度约为74-75%；跨独立来源的相互印证显著提高了量化精度。该数据库以CC-BY许可协议及完整源代码开源发布。

Conclusion: 通过聚合独立来源而非依赖单一检测方法，OpenPVMapper达到了单个来源无法企及的信心水平，并且具有可扩展性，可随着新数据源的可用而继续扩展。这为光伏监测和政策制定提供了高质量的基础设施。

Abstract: Rooftop photovoltaic (PV) systems account for the vast majority of PV grid connections, yet no open, comprehensive, installation-level dataset of these systems exists: public registries aggregate data only above a capacity threshold, and remote sensing-based detection efforts, while extensive, are typically confined to a single method, a limited geographic scope, or a single point in time. We introduce OpenPVMapper, a nationwide, multi-source database of rooftop PV installations in mainland France, built by aggregating and reconciling complementary sources: a deep learning-based detection pipeline deployed on nationwide aerial imagery, OpenStreetMap and a probabilistic building-level detection dataset. The resulting database contains 1,135,850 installations, totaling approximately 15.01~GWp of installed capacity, each documented with its provenance, detection method, and, where available, a manual validation flag. Manual review of a stratified sample of 1,862 installations places the database's overall precision at approximately 74--75\%, with corroboration across independent sources bringing a substantial, quantified precision gain. By aggregating independent sources rather than relying on any single detection method, OpenPVMapper reaches a level of confidence beyond what any one source could provide on its own, while remaining extensible to further sources as they become available. It is released under an open (CC-BY) license alongside the full source code used to build it.

</details>


### [16] [OrganLens: Organ-Specific Representation Learning for CT Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25164)
*Zhixuan Ge,Anqi Li,Sadeer Al-Kindi,Hanwen Xu,Wei Qiu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出OrganLens，通过自监督学习利用共享编码器生成特定器官的CT表示，无需外部分割掩码，在多种下游任务中显著优于全局表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CT基础模型通常产生单一的全局体积表示，而现有的解剖感知方法要么移除相关上下文（预分离器官），要么未在特征形成前以选定器官为条件（显式解耦）。这导致难以针对特定器官的异常、预后或纵向变化进行有效建模。

Method: 引入OrganLens框架：1) 使用器官身份作为条件输入到共享CT编码器；2) 采用器官特异性蒸馏和解剖掩码监督来塑造特征；3) 通过解剖加权池化生成器官特异性表示。推理时无需外部分割掩码即可从共享模型输出11种器官特异性表示。

Result: 在CT-RATE、RAD-ChestCT、INSPECT和NLST数据集上评估：相比CT预训练的DINOv2，心脏表示将CT-RATE心肌肥大AUROC从0.910提升至0.953；肺部表示使NLST肺癌死亡率的Harrell C-index提高14.2%；全局表示在INSPECT文本-图像/图像-文本检索中Recall@10分别达到33.09%和32.04%。

Conclusion: 解剖匹配的特异性表示提供了更强的任务相关信号，而全局表示保留了广泛用途。OrganLens提供了一种可扩展的方法，用于医学研究社区跨队列和临床终点研究器官特异性疾病。

Abstract: A CT examination captures multiple organs, but many biomedical questions concern abnormalities, prognosis, or longitudinal change in a specific organ. These questions require a separate representation for each organ within the same CT volume. Existing CT foundation models commonly produce a single volume-level representation, while recent anatomy-aware methods either encode pre-separated organ volumes or explicitly disentangle images into organ token groups. The former may remove clinically relevant surrounding context, while the latter does not condition a shared encoder on a selected organ before its features are formed. We introduce OrganLens for organ-specific representation learning through self-supervision. An organ identity conditions a shared CT encoder, while organ-specific distillation and anatomy-mask supervision shape features for anatomy-weighted pooling into organ-specific representations. At inference, the shared model produces 11 organ-specific representations without external segmentation masks. We evaluate OrganLens on CT-RATE, RAD-ChestCT, INSPECT, and NLST across diverse acquisitions and downstream evaluations. Relative to CT-pretrained DINOv2, heart representations raise CT-RATE cardiomegaly AUROC from 0.910 to 0.953, while lung representations improve the Harrell C-index for NLST lung-cancer mortality by 14.2\%. The global representation reaches INSPECT Recall@10 of 33.09\% and 32.04\% for text-to-image and image-to-text retrieval, respectively. Across organ-related tasks, anatomically matched representations provide stronger task-relevant signal, while the global representation retains broad utility. OrganLens offers a scalable approach to organ-specific CT representation learning with a shared encoder. More broadly, it provides the medical research community with a reusable framework for studying organ-specific disease across cohorts and clinical endpoints.

</details>


### [17] [LGFNet: A CTC-Guided Local-Global Fusion Framework for Single-Channel Sleep Staging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25197)
*Chongjian Wang,Zhenghang Hou,Junjie Gao,Xiaofang Zhong,Shiyuan Han,Tong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LGFNet，一种基于CTC引导的序列到序列框架，用于解决单通道睡眠分期中的长程依赖、阶段转换模糊及分布偏移问题，在多个基准数据集上优于现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 睡眠分期面临长程时间依赖性、N1期等阶段转换模糊、以及不同受试者/采样率/脑电导联间的显著分布偏移挑战；尤其在可穿戴设备所需的单通道、低延迟场景下，这些问题更加突出。

Method: 提出LGFNet框架：1) 局部-全局融合编码器（Local-Global Fusion encoder）联合建模细粒度时间动态与长程睡眠结构；2) CTC-Attention联合训练范式统一时间对齐与上下文依赖建模；3) 三阶段解码策略，结合CTC引导解码和Viterbi平滑以减少误差累积并保证生理一致性。

Result: 在五个公共基准数据集上进行跨数据集评估，LGFNet consistently 优于现有的单通道最先进方法。在Sleep-EDF-78数据集上，相比DMIN模型，准确率提升+1.27%，宏F1提升+1.74%，Kappa系数提升+1.93%，特别是在N1期和过渡段表现显著改善。

Conclusion: LGFNet具有强大的鲁棒性和泛化能力，能有效应对不同采样率、导联配置和记录环境下的睡眠分期任务，适用于单通道实时应用场景。

Abstract: Sleep staging remains challenging due to long-range temporal dependencies, ambiguous stage transitions-particularly in N1-and substantial distribution shifts across subjects, sampling rates, and EEG montages. These difficulties are further amplified in single-channel, low-latency scenarios required by wearable and real-world applications. To address these issues, we propose LGFNet, a CTC-guided sequence-to-sequence framework for robust sleep staging. LGFNet introduces a Local-Global Fusion encoder that jointly models fine-grained temporal dynamics and long-range sleep structure, overcoming the limitations of conventional serial hybrid architectures. A CTC-Attention joint training paradigm is adopted to unify temporal alignment with context-dependent modeling, enabling more accurate recognition of stage boundaries and transitions. Furthermore, a three-stage decoding strategy is devised, leveraging CTC-guided decoding and Viterbi-based smoothing to reduce error accumulation and enforce physiological consistency. Extensive cross-dataset evaluations on five public benchmarks demonstrate that LGFNet consistently outperforms state-of-the-art single-channel methods. In particular, on Sleep-EDF-78, LGFNet surpasses DMIN by +1.27% accuracy, +1.74% macro-F1, and +1.93% kappa, with pronounced gains on N1 and transition segments, highlighting its robustness and strong generalization across diverse sampling rates, montages, and recording environments.

</details>


### [18] [ObliCity: A Benchmark and Baseline for Roof-to-Ground Projection Displacement Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25210)
*Kai Li,Yupeng Deng,Ligao Deng,Zhihao Xi,Chenhao Wang,Jierui Zhang,Yingrui Ji,Yu Meng,Xiangyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DragRoof模型和ObliCity数据集，通过显式学习屋顶到地基的偏移向量（RFOV）来解决斜视遥感图像中的几何投影畸变问题，实现了几何对齐与语义分割的解耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么忽略斜视影像中建筑屋顶与地基间的几何投影位移，要么在基于分割的框架中隐式处理，导致进展主要依赖通用分割技术的提升而非几何校正本身的改进。

Method: 1. 构建ObliCity数据集：首个整合高分辨率无人机影像和全球卫星数据的大规模基准，涵盖多样城市形态和视角；2. 提出DragRoof框架：将RFOV提取定义为独立学习任务，基于ODE模拟人工拖动屋顶至地基的过程，学习确定性、几何一致的偏移场，并通过End Token自适应确定收敛。

Result: 在ObliCity数据集上，DragRoof取得了最先进的RFOV提取性能，不仅推理步骤更少，且在方向性和长度精度上表现更优。

Conclusion: 该研究为斜视遥感影像中的投影位移校正提供了原则性基础，发布了开源代码和数据集。

Abstract: Oblique-view urban remote sensing imagery inevitably exhibits geometric projection displacements between building roofs and footprints, leading to significant distortions in spatial structure. Existing approaches either ignore these deformations or handle them implicitly within segmentation-based frameworks, where progress is dominated by general segmentation advances rather than improvements in geometric correction. In this work, we explicitly define roof-to-footprint offset vector (RFOV) extraction as an independent learning task that decouples geometric alignment from semantic segmentation. To support this task, we introduce the Oblique City dataset (ObliCity), the first large-scale benchmark that integrates high-resolution UAV imagery and globally distributed satellite data, covering diverse city morphologies and camera perspectives. Methodologically, we reformulate DragOSM into DragRoof, an ODE-based framework inspired by human annotation behavior. By simulating the continuous process of dragging roofs toward their footprints, DragRoof learns deterministic, geometry-consistent offset fields and adaptively determines convergence through an end token. Extensive experiments on ObliCity demonstrate that DragRoof achieves state-of-the-art RFOV extraction performance, requiring fewer inference steps while delivering superior directional and length accuracy. Our dataset and model establish a principled foundation for studying projection displacement correction in oblique remote sensing imagery. The source code and dataset will be avaliable at https://github.com/likaiucas/DragRoof.

</details>


### [19] [WHTMix: Efficient Stereo Depth Estimation via Walsh-Hadamard Token Mixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25234)
*Prathyush Sajith,Emadeldeen Hamdan,Ahmet Enis Cetin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于Walsh-Hadamard变换的Token Mixer替代立体匹配Transformer中的全局自注意力机制，以在保持精度的同时显著降低计算成本和推理延迟，特别适用于高分辨率场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于Transformer的立体匹配方法中，全局自注意力机制的计算复杂度随像素数量呈二次方增长，导致在高分辨率、低延迟要求的应用（如自动驾驶、机器人、增强现实）中运行时性能受限。

Method: 1. 使用数据无关的Walsh-Hadamard Token Mixer替换立体Transformer中的联合自注意力阶段，在全局范围内以对数线性成本混合Token；2. 保留执行左右视图对应关系的数据依赖交叉注意力；3. 引入混合对数视差损失函数（hybrid log-disparity loss），以提高远距离小视差像素的权重。

Result: 在合成驾驶数据上，该方法在终点误差上与注意力基线相当，但模型计算量减少了2.46倍，单图像推理延迟降低了2.65倍。复杂度分析表明，该优势取决于序列长度与通道宽度的比率，解释了为何高分辨率立体匹配是理想应用场景。混合损失函数在不增加计算开销的情况下减少了对远处物体的误差。

Conclusion: 通过用Walsh-Hadamard Token Mixer替代全局自注意力，并结合特定的损失函数设计，可以在高分辨率立体匹配任务中实现高效且高精度的性能，验证了Token-to-Channel缩放比例在长序列非立体基准测试中的普遍适用性。

Abstract: Stereo depth estimation for driving, robotics and augmented reality must run at high resolution under tight latency budgets, yet in transformer-based matchers the global self-attention that aggregates scene context grows quadratically with the number of pixels and comes to dominate runtime. We show that the joint self-attention stage of a stereo transformer, whose role is to spread context across both views, can be replaced by a data-independent Walsh-Hadamard token mixer that mixes tokens globally in the transform domain at log-linear cost, while the data-dependent cross-attention that performs left-right correspondence is retained. On synthetic driving data the mixer matches the attention baseline in end-point error while reducing model compute by a factor of 2.46 and single-image inference latency by a factor of 2.65. A complexity analysis shows the benefit is governed by the ratio of sequence length to channel width, which explains why high-resolution stereo matching is a particularly favorable setting and why classification transformers are not; we confirm this token-to-channel scaling on non-stereo long-sequence benchmarks. Furthermore, we introduce a hybrid log-disparity loss function designed to up-weight small-disparity pixels corresponding to long-range objects. This approach reduces the error on distant objects without incurring any additional computational overhead.

</details>


### [20] [CD-RMOT-Bench: Benchmarking the Cross-Domain Referring Multi-Object Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25239)
*Xiangqun Zhang,Likai Wang,Zekun Qian,Ruize Han,Wei Feng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了跨域指代多目标跟踪（CD-RMOT）问题，即训练好的模型如何在视觉条件变化的无标签目标域中遵循自然语言指令。为此构建了CD-RMOT-Bench基准测试，并提出了查询中心适应（QCA）框架以稳定视觉轨迹与语言表达之间的关联，实验表明域偏移严重损害性能，主要源于不稳定的时空关联而非单纯的检测错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的指代多目标跟踪（RMOT）研究主要在域内设置下进行，缺乏对语言条件跟踪在不可避免视觉域偏移下鲁棒性的探索。为了评估训练有素的RMOT模型能否在具有不同视觉条件的无标签目标域中可靠地遵循自然语言表达式，需要研究跨域指代多目标跟踪这一新且具有挑战性的问题。

Method: 1. 构建CD-RMOT-Bench统一基准：结合真实清晰域指代跟踪数据、对齐的数字孪生变体以及真实恶劣域视频，支持受控的天气/视角偏移分析和现实合成-真实转移评估。
2. 提出查询中心适应（QCA）框架：旨在稳定连接视觉轨迹和指代表达式的查询空间。

Result: 1. CD-RMOT-Bench为跨域鲁棒性语言引导跟踪提供了系统研究的平台。
2. QCA建立了强有力的基线性能。
3. 广泛实验显示，域偏移严重降低RMOT性能；失败不仅由物体检测错误引起，更关键的是由不稳定的表达条件时空关联和目标选择导致。

Conclusion: CD-RMOT是一个新的挑战性问题，现有的RMOT模型在域偏移下表现不佳。通过构建CD-RMOT-Bench基准和提出QCA框架，本文为跨视觉域的鲁棒语言引导跟踪开辟了新方向，强调了稳定时空关联的重要性。

Abstract: Referring multi-object tracking (RMOT) extends tracking from category-driven perception to language-guided understanding by grounding object trajectories in natural-language expressions. Despite recent progress, existing RMOT studies are largely conducted under in-domain settings, leaving the robustness of language-conditioned tracking under inevitable visual domain shifts unexplored. In this paper, we study Cross-Domain Referring Multi-Object Tracking (CD-RMOT), a new and challenging problem that evaluates whether an RMOT model trained on a labeled source domain can reliably follow natural-language expressions in an unlabeled target domain with different visual conditions. To support systematic study, we construct CD-RMOT-Bench, a unified benchmark that combines real clear-domain referring tracking data, aligned digital-twin variants, and real adverse-domain videos. CD-RMOT-Bench enables both controlled weather/viewpoint shift analysis and realistic synthetic-real transfer evaluation under a shared RMOT protocol. Further, we provide a Query-Centric Adaptation (QCA) framework, designed to stabilize the query space that bridges visual trajectories and referring expressions. Extensive experiments reveal that domain shifts severely degrade RMOT performance, where the failure is not merely caused by object detection errors but more critically by unstable expression-conditioned temporal association and target selection. QCA establishes a strong baseline, while CD-RMOT-Bench opens a new direction for robust language-guided tracking across visual domains.

</details>


### [21] [Medical world models in healthcare: foundations, applications, and challenges for trustworthy clinical translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25242)
*Zhaoyan Chen,Zhongxiu Cong,Zhuanfeng Jin,Wanshu Fan,Dongsheng Zhou,Qi Ai,Haifan Gong,Congyu Liao,Xiaofeng Liu,Cong Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文综述了医疗世界模型（Medical World Models），定义了其概念边界、技术基础及应用领域。通过筛选148项研究，提炼出四大核心能力：患者状态表征、时间动态建模、干预条件模拟及临床监督规划。尽管在轨迹预测和干预对比上展示了技术可行性，但当前研究多处于回顾性或临床前阶段，存在数据不完整、因果识别有限等挑战。未来转化需依赖精确的干预表示、因果机制 grounding 及前瞻性多中心验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在扩展医疗人工智能 beyond static prediction（超越静态预测），通过建模患者状态随时间的演变及对临床干预的反应，为医疗AI提供动态框架；同时界定该领域的概念边界、技术要求及证据标准。

Method: 采用结构化叙事综合（structured narrative synthesis）与可复现的证据映射方法。从1,455条独特记录中筛选并汇编98个来源，其中14项研究符合严格的实证定义。围绕四大能力维度对现有文献进行系统梳理和分析。

Result: 1. **领域架构**：确立以患者状态表征、时间动态建模、干预条件模拟、临床监督规划为核心的四大能力框架，将临床数字孪生视为跨领域整合框架。
2. **应用范围**：涵盖医学影像、纵向电子健康记录、治疗反应建模、生理/多模态状态建模、超声/手术交互及人群/卫生系统模拟。
3. **现状评估**：提供了轨迹预测和候选干预措施比较的早期技术可行性证据，但多数研究仍为回顾性、特定任务或临床前性质。
4. **局限性**：指出证据基础受限于纵向干预数据不完整、行动语义不一致、因果可识别性有限、长视界误差累积、不确定性估计不足及外部验证有限。

Conclusion: 医疗世界模型的临床转化依赖于实现精确的干预表示、稳健的因果与机制 grounding、校准过的轨迹级不确定性、安全约束的规划策略，以及针对具有临床意义终点的前瞻性多中心验证。

Abstract: Medical world models offer a framework for extending medical artificial intelligence beyond static prediction by representing evolving patient states and modelling how they change over time and in response to clinical interventions. This Review defines the conceptual boundaries, technical foundations, application domains, and evidence requirements of the field through a structured narrative synthesis with reproducible evidence mapping.We screened 1,455 unique records and assembled a corpus of 98 sources, including 14 studies that met a strict empirical definition of a medical world model. The field is organised around four capabilities: patient state representation, temporal dynamics modelling, intervention-conditioned simulation, and clinician-supervised planning. Evidence spans medical imaging, longitudinal electronic health records, treatment response modelling, physiological and multimodal state modelling, ultrasound and surgical interaction, and population and health-system simulation; clinical digital twins are treated as a cross-cutting integration framework.Current studies provide early evidence of technical feasibility for trajectory forecasting and comparison of candidate interventions, but most remain retrospective, task-specific, or preclinical. The evidence base is further limited by incomplete longitudinal intervention data, inconsistent action semantics, limited causal identifiability, long-horizon error accumulation, inadequate uncertainty estimation, and limited external validation. Clinical translation will therefore depend on precise intervention representations, robust causal and mechanistic grounding, calibrated trajectory-level uncertainty, safety-constrained planning, and prospective multicentre validation against clinically meaningful endpoints.

</details>


### [22] [FORGE: Frame Orthogonality in Relevance Geometry for Long-Form Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25266)
*Ghazal Kaviani,Ghassan AlRegib*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FORGE，一种无需训练的模型无关方法，通过在预训练多模态嵌入空间中诱导查询条件几何结构，最大化推理时所选帧子集的查询相关信息密度，显著提升长视频理解性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）虽支持长视频理解，但随着视频序列长度增加，相关内容密度急剧下降，暴露于过多无关内容会显著降低模型准确率。

Method: 提出FORGE（Frame Orthogonality in Relevance Geometry），一种模型无关的推理时关键帧选择方法。该方法在预训练多模态嵌入空间中构建查询条件的几何结构，将相关性与多样性统一为目标函数，通过选取覆盖独立查询相关方向的帧来捕获多样化且高信息量的内容。

Result: 在Video-MME和LongVideoBench基准测试中（预算为16、32、64帧），FORGE的关键帧选择得分比最强免训练基线提高11.0-15.3分；在Video-MME上K=64时关键帧召回率翻倍（0.415 vs 0.204）。在8个开源MLLMs（4B至32B参数）上的问答任务显示，准确率较均匀采样提升最高8.7分，较最强基线提升5.2分。

Conclusion: 将嵌入空间与查询的高维结构对齐，是提升推理时视频理解能力的一个有前景的方向。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have enabled long-form video understanding at a scale that was not previously possible. However, the density of relevant content decreases sharply as video sequence length increases, and exposing the model to more irrelevant content measurably reduces its accuracy. In this paper, we address the problem of maximizing query-relevant information in a frame subset selected at inference time, without training. FORGE (Frame Orthogonality in Relevance Geometry) is a model-agnostic method that induces a query-conditioned geometry on a pretrained multimodal embedding space, unifying relevance and diversity into a single objective. In this space, frames that cover independent query-relevant directions are far apart, and selecting the subset of maximum information captures diverse query-relevant content within the budget. Experiments on Video-MME and LongVideoBench at budgets of 16, 32, and 64 frames show that FORGE improves the unified keyframe selection score by 11.0-15.3 points over the strongest training-free baseline and up to doubles keyframe recall (0.415 vs. 0.204 at K=64 on Video-MME). The gains extend to question answering, where accuracy improves in every evaluated setting across eight open-source MLLMs spanning 4B to 32B parameters, by up to 8.7 points over uniform sampling and 5.2 points over the strongest baseline. Our findings suggest that aligning the embedding space with the query's high-dimensional structure is a promising direction for inference-time video understanding.

</details>


### [23] [ScaleResfusion: Residual Rectified Flow based on Residual Vector Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25275)
*Zhenning Shi,Chen Xu,Junhao Zhang,Kefei Zhang,Linjie Liu,Zhedong Zheng,Tao Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ScaleResfusion，一种基于预训练文本到图像整流流模型的可扩展扩散框架。核心创新为残差整流流（Residual Rectified Flow），通过从含噪低质图像出发而非纯噪声，实现高效、保真且参数高效的微调，结合知识蒸馏降低采样成本，在真实世界图像恢复任务中达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的图像恢复方法存在两大挑战：1）从高斯噪声起始的方法速度慢且对输入保真度低；2）基于残差的方法通常从头训练，难以利用现代预生成先验。

Method: 构建ScaleResfusion框架，核心是引入残差项R的“残差整流流”。该方法使用从含噪低质图像开始的残差传输路径，具有精确加速点。通过学习残差向量场，保持输出分布和线性扩散过程与预训练模型一致，支持大规模参数高效微调。此外，引入知识蒸馏管道以降低采样成本。

Result: 在多个真实世界图像恢复任务上进行广泛实验，ScaleResfusion实现了最先进的性能，同时显著提高了效率。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一种实用且可扩展的方法，将大型预训练扩散模型适配到真实世界图像恢复任务中。

Abstract: Real-world Image Restoration (Real-IR) aims to recover high-quality (HQ) images from complex and unknown degradations. Although recent diffusion-based methods have substantially improved perceptual quality, their current designs leave two key challenges unresolved. Methods that start from Gaussian noise are slow and often less faithful to the degraded input. Residual-based methods usually train from scratch, which makes it hard to exploit modern pre-trained generative priors. In this paper, we present ScaleResfusion, a scalable diffusion framework for real-world image restoration built on pre-trained text-to-image rectified-flow models. The core of our method is Residual Rectified Flow, which introduces the residual term R into Standard Rectified Flow. Instead of starting from pure noise, it uses a residual transport path that starts from noisy low-quality (LQ) images and admits an exact acceleration point. By learning the residual vector field, Residual Rectified Flow keeps the output distribution and linear diffusion process consistent with the pre-trained rectified-flow models. This makes parameter-efficient fine-tuning possible at scale. We further introduce a knowledge-distillation pipeline to reduce sampling cost while maintaining restoration quality. Extensive experiments on multiple real-world restoration tasks show that ScaleResfusion achieves state-of-the-art performance with much higher efficiency. These results suggest a practical and scalable way to adapt large pre-trained diffusion models to real-world image restoration. Our code and models are available at https://github.com/YukinoshitaLove/ScaleResfusion.

</details>


### [24] [FunnelAL: Retrieve-then-Rank Active Learning for Single-Class Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25276)
*Reihaneh Rostami,Brian Goodwin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FunnelAL是一种用于单类发现的检索-排序主动学习系统，借鉴工业推荐系统的漏斗架构，通过多阶段迭代（嵌入检索、精度触发排序、专家反馈）显著降低标注成本并提高分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模监督学习面临两大挑战：在海量语料中高效查找相关样本，以及在嵌入无法清晰分离类别时区分真阳性与视觉混淆的负样本。传统主动学习作为单阶段过程，未能高效解决上述问题。

Method: 提出FunnelAL系统，将问题分解为级联阶段：(1) 基于嵌入的检索评分以缩小候选集；(2) 利用RankNet进行排序，当批次精度下降时自动融合委员会查询(QBC)进行探索；(3) 根据标注者反馈迭代优化前两个阶段。

Result: 在三个图像分类基准测试中，FunnelAL实现了最高的最终F1分数、最优的标注效率（AULC排名第一）和最少的标注轮次。相比最新方法（GAL, PF-MA），其质量相当但标注成本更低。在存在现实标注错误率的情况下，FunnelAL表现优于退化为两到三倍的经典基于不确定性方法。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了多阶段推荐系统与主动学习之间的具体桥梁，证明了漏斗架构在数据标注中的有效性。

Abstract: We present FunnelAL, a retrieve-then-rank active learning system for single-class discovery, which adapts the multi-stage funnel architecture of industrial recommender systems to data annotation. Large-scale supervised learning faces two challenges: efficiently finding relevant samples in a massive corpus, and distinguishing true positives from visually confusable negatives when embeddings do not cleanly separate classes. Conventional active learning offers a principled framework for reducing annotation cost, yet it treats sample selection as a single-stage process that addresses neither challenge efficiently. FunnelAL decomposes the problem into cascaded stages. Starting from a single positive and negative example, the system iterates through: (1) embedding-based retrieval scoring that narrows the corpus to a manageable candidate set; (2) a precision-triggered ranking stage that exploits a learned ranker (RankNet) while batch precision remains high, then automatically blends in committee-based exploration (QBC) once returns diminish; and (3) feedback from the annotator's labels that refines both stages in subsequent iterations. We evaluate on three diverse image classification benchmarks. With a perfect annotator, FunnelAL attains the best final F1 on all three benchmarks, the best annotation efficiency (first in AULC), and the fewest annotation rounds. The most recent single-class discovery methods (GAL, PF-MA) at best match its final quality, and only at consistently higher labeling cost. Under annotator labeling errors at realistic rates, FunnelAL remains first or statistically tied for first while classical uncertainty-based methods degrade two to three times faster. Our work provides a concrete bridge between multi-stage recommender systems and active learning.

</details>


### [25] [CLBench-V: Evaluating Multimodal Context Learning from Grounding to Knowledge Acquisition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25294)
*Lai Wei,Chengqi Li,Jiapeng Li,Ruina Hu,Yue Wang,Weiran Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出多模态上下文学习基准CLBench-V，涵盖科学、金融、空间推理等领域，评估显示当前模型性能仍远未饱和（最高分仅0.2847）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文学习评估主要局限于文本，而实际应用场景中上下文多为多模态（如图表、地图、网页），缺乏针对多模态上下文学习能力的系统性评估。

Method: 构建CLBench-V基准，从上下文定位、新信息应用、新知识学习三个维度组织任务；结合转换的公开基准与新构建的数据集，采用自动化流程降低构建成本。

Result: 在3,443个实例和六个最新多模态模型上测试，最佳总分仅为0.2847；InternVL3.5-30B-A3B在上下文定位和新知识学习上表现最佳，Qwen3.5-Plus在新信息应用上领先。

Conclusion: 多模态上下文学习仍存在巨大提升空间，尚未达到饱和状态，需进一步改进模型对多模态上下文的利用能力。

Abstract: Real-world tasks often require models to learn from task-specific context rather than relying only on pre-trained knowledge. While recent work has highlighted this capability as context learning, existing evaluations mainly focus on textual contexts. In many practical settings, however, the context to be learned from is multimodal: scientific findings are conveyed through figures and tables, financial indicators are scattered across converted reports, and spatial decisions depend on maps, scenes, or web pages. We introduce CLBench-V, a benchmark for multimodal context learning that addresses the difficulty of localizing where context use breaks down by organizing tasks around three dimensions: context grounding, new information application, and new knowledge learning. CLBench-V combines converted public benchmarks with newly constructed datasets spanning domains such as science, finance, long-document understanding, spatial reasoning, and web-based visual question answering. To reduce the cost of constructing domain-specific context-learning tasks, we further use automated construction and filtering procedures for our newly built datasets. Across 3,443 instances and six recent multimodal models, the best overall score is only 0.2847, indicating that multimodal context learning remains far from saturated. Moreover, InternVL3.5-30B-A3B performs best on context grounding and new knowledge learning, while Qwen3.5-Plus performs best on new information application. We further analyze judge reliability, context length, image count, and representative failure cases. Code is available at https://github.com/IamLihua/CLBench-V.

</details>


### [26] [MEDit-Bench: A Dataset for Evaluating Message-Driven Narrative Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25300)
*Katsuya Ogata,Zongshang Pang,Mayu Otani,Yuta Nakashima*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MEDit-Bench基准，用于评估以消息为导向的视频编辑。发现现有LLM-as-a-judge存在严重位置偏差不可靠，且模型在严格标准下仍显著落后于人类专业编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频摘要基准将编辑意图简化为单一的消息无关显著性，无法反映同一素材因叙事目标不同而产生的多样化剪辑结果，缺乏对“以消息为导向”的视频编辑的有效评估手段。

Method: 构建MEDit-Bench数据集，包含长视频、多种编辑消息及对应的专业剪辑版本；定义基于时间对齐指标的自动评估协议；标注消息的模糊性和上下文充分性得分；使用多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）和强化微调基线进行实验；开展人类感知研究。

Result: 1. 不同消息导致相同源素材产生显著不同的剪辑；2. LLM-as-a-judge因严重位置偏差而不可靠；3. 消息的模糊性和上下文充分性与模型性能负相关，可作为难度分层依据；4. 强模型在宽松阈值下接近人类时间对齐水平，但在严格标准下仍落后；5. 人类专业编辑在质量上持续优于模型输出。

Conclusion: 当前多模态模型在以消息为导向的视频编辑任务中仍存在明显能力差距，尤其在严格叙事质量和时间对齐方面远逊于人类专家，需改进评估方法并提升模型对复杂叙事意图的理解与执行能力。

Abstract: Video editing is fundamentally message-driven: even from the same source footage, the selected shots change depending on the narrative the editor wishes to convey. Benchmarks for a closely related task, video summarization, reduce editorial intent to a single, message-agnostic notion of saliency and thus do not account for this diversity. For evaluating message-driven video editing, we present \textbf{MEDit-Bench}, a dataset and benchmark, which pairs long-form videos with multiple editing messages and multiple professionally produced edits per message, demonstrating that different messages yield substantially different edits from the same source. We define an automatic evaluation protocol based on temporal alignment metrics, and find that an LLM-as-a-judge preference, a natural proxy for narrative quality, is unreliable for this task due to severe position bias. We additionally annotate each message with ambiguity and contextfulness scores, and show that both dimensions negatively correlate with model performance, establishing message difficulty as a meaningful stratification factor. Experiments with state-of-the-art MLLMs and reinforcement fine-tuned baselines show that while strong models approach human temporal alignment at lenient thresholds, all models fall behind humans at stricter criteria. A human perceptual study further confirms a large quality gap, with professional human edits remaining consistently preferred over model outputs.

</details>


### [27] [Human-in-the-Loop Signature Bootstrapping for UAV Hyperspectral PFM-1 Mine Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25310)
*Sagar Lekhak,Prasanna Reddy Pulakurthi,Emmett J. Ientilucci*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了在无人机可见光/近红外高光谱成像中检测PFM-1地雷的性能，重点评估了不同算法（SAM、MF、ACE、CEM）和签名获取方式对误报率和目标发现效率的影响。结果表明，自适应相干估计器（ACE）在减少人工审核工作量方面显著优于其他方法，仅需9次候选检查即可确认所有目标区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高光谱成像虽可用于材料识别，但在实际排雷作业中，操作员需要花费大量时间审核误报以找到真实目标。因此，研究如何降低“发现目标前需审核的误报数量”至关重要。

Method: 使用无人机可见光/近红外高光谱成像系统，针对PFM-1地雷进行检测实验。比较了四种检测算法：光谱角映射器（SAM）、匹配滤波器（MF）、自适应相干估计器（ACE）和约束能量最小化（CEM）。同时对比了三种目标签名获取策略：地面测量SVC签名、完全知情场景核心像素签名、以及模拟人机回环引导的签名自举方法。评价指标包括ROC曲线下面积、平均精度、目标发现曲线及空间候选审核计数。

Result: 全审查自举法在验证完全部七个目标区域后能达到完全知情场景签名的性能水平。然而，所需的人工审核努力程度差异巨大：ACE算法仅需两轮检查和九次候选审核即可确认所有区域；而基于SAM的方法则需要数千次候选审核才能定位最终目标。

Conclusion: 在无人机高光谱地雷检测应用中，选择合适的检测算法（如ACE）能极大降低操作员的认知负荷和审核成本，提高实战可行性。代码已开源。

Abstract: Hyperspectral imaging (HSI) is useful for material discrimination, but operational mine screening also depends on how many false alarms must be inspected before targets are found. This paper studies PFM-1 landmine detection in unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) visible and near-infrared (VNIR) HSI using spectral angle mapper (SAM), matched filter (MF), adaptive coherence estimator (ACE), and constrained energy minimization (CEM). We compare a ground-measured SVC signature, a fully informed in-scene core-pixel signature, and a simulated human-in-the-loop signature bootstrap. Besides receiver operating characteristic area under the curve and average precision, we report target-discovery curves and spatial candidate-review counts. Full-review bootstrapping reaches the fully informed in-scene signature case after all seven target regions are verified, but the required inspection effort varies strongly: ACE confirms all regions in two rounds and nine candidate inspections, whereas the SAM variants need thousands of candidate reviews for their final target locations. Code is available at https://github.com/SagarLekhak/IEEE_WHISPERS_2026_UAV_HSI_PFM1.

</details>


### [28] [Sense it with your eyes: Sensation Generation and Understanding for Advertisements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25314)
*Aysan Aghazadeh,Sina Malakouti,Adriana Kovashka*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究首次系统性地探讨了感官广告的理解、评估与生成。通过构建Sensory Ad数据集、提出SenseClass基准任务、开发SenseScore自动评估指标以及设计多智能体框架SAGA，填补了AI在视觉感官唤起领域的空白，为基于视觉的说服力传播奠定了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管人工智能在生成和理解创意及说服性内容方面日益普及，但广告如何通过视觉线索唤起人类感官体验这一领域仍 largely unexplored（鲜有探索）。现有研究缺乏对感官广告的系统性理解和评估工具。

Method: 1. 构建Sensory Ad数据集；2. 定义感觉分类任务（SenseClass）以基准测试大语言模型（LLMs）和多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）；3. 提出SenseScore，一种能与人判断高度一致的自动化感官唤起评估指标；4. 引入感官广告生成（SenseGen）任务，并提出多智能体框架SAGA，旨在优化消息-图像对齐、感官唤起和说服力。

Result: 成功建立了感官广告的评估体系（SenseScore），并提出了有效的生成框架（SAGA）。SAGA在多智能体协作下，显著提升了广告中信息内容与图像的匹配度、感官唤起的强度以及整体的说服效果。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了“感官感知视觉说服力”的研究基础，展示了AI在理解和生成具有感官唤起能力的广告方面的潜力，为未来的相关研究和应用提供了重要的基准和工具。

Abstract: Sensory advertising evokes human senses through visual cues, enabling audiences to mentally simulate experiences and increasing persuasive impact. Despite the recent increase in using AI in generating and understanding creative and persuasive content, how advertisements visually evoke sensations remains largely unexplored. In this work, we introduce the first study of understanding, evaluating, and generating sensory ads. We introduce the Sensory Ad dataset, and define sensation classification tasks (SenseClass) to benchmark LLMs and MLLMs. We further propose SenseScore, an automated evaluation metric for sensation evocation achieving strong agreement with human judgments. Finally, we introduce the Sensory Ad Generation (SenseGen) task and propose SAGA, a multi-agent framework that improves message image alignment, sensory evocation, and persuasion. Our work establishes a foundation for sensory-aware visual persuasion.

</details>


### [29] [Beyond Background Bias: Saliency-Driven Prototype Alignment for Dataset Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25318)
*Yawen Zou,Wenqi Cai,Guang Li,Ling Xiao,Chunzhi Gu,Chao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种显著性驱动的扩散模型蒸馏框架，通过构建类判别性潜在原型来解决现有方法在复杂视觉领域中结构连贯性和泛化能力不足的问题。该方法利用集成Grad-CAM提取高置信度区域作为原型，并通过硬原型细化增强判别力与多样性，同时保持扩散骨干网络冻结，仅训练轻量级分类器，在多基准测试中显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于扩散的蒸馏方法在视觉上复杂的领域中往往难以保持结构连贯性和泛化能力。这主要源于潜在原型与类判别性区域对齐较弱，且受到无关背景的污染，从而降低了生成质量和泛化性能。

Method: 提出了一个显著性驱动的蒸馏框架，包含两个阶段：(1) 使用集成Grad-CAM显著性来构建强调高置信度区域的类判别性潜在原型；(2) 应用硬原型细化（hard prototype refinement）以构建具有挑战性但类一致的原型，从而增强判别力和多样性。在此过程中，扩散骨干网络（如LDM和DiT）保持冻结，仅训练用于显著性提取的轻量级分类器。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上进行了广泛实验，结果表明该方法相比强基线方法具有一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 该框架通过显著性引导的原型构建有效提升了数据蒸馏的代表性和泛化能力，为降低计算和存储成本提供了一种高效解决方案。代码将开源。

Abstract: Dataset distillation aims to synthesize compact datasets that can approximate the performance of full-data training while significantly reducing computational and storage costs. However, diffusion-based distillation methods often struggle to preserve structural coherence and generalization, especially in visually complex domains. This issue often stems from latent prototypes that are weakly aligned with class-discriminative regions and contaminated by irrelevant background, thereby degrading generation quality and generalization. To address this limitation, we propose a saliency-driven distillation framework that constructs class-discriminative latent prototypes to enhance representativeness and generalization. The framework proceeds in two stages: (1) ensemble Grad-CAM saliency is used to construct prototypes emphasizing high-confidence regions, and (2) hard prototype refinement is then applied to construct challenging yet class-consistent prototypes, thereby enhancing discriminability and diversity. Importantly, the diffusion backbones (e.g., LDM and DiT) remain frozen; only lightweight classifiers used for saliency extraction are trained. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks demonstrate consistent performance improvements over strong baselines. Code will be released.

</details>


### [30] [Balanced Soft mixture-of-expert model for Glaucoma Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25324)
*Sai Venkatesh Chilukoti,Krishna Rauniyar,Min Shi,Xiali Hei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种平衡软混合专家模型，通过三个专家和负载均衡损失解决多模态学习中单模态表示不平衡的问题，在青光眼检测上超越了所有基线模型，并具备向糖尿病视网膜病变等其他疾病检测泛化的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 青光眼导致不可逆视力丧失且早期难以察觉，早期检测至关重要。虽然深度学习单模态模型已提升检测精度，但新兴的多模态模型因联合学习目标导致单模态表示不平衡和优化不足，亟需改进以提升鲁棒性和准确性。

Method: 提出一种平衡软混合专家（balanced soft mixture-experts）模型，包含三个专家网络，并引入负载均衡损失（load balancing loss）来优化多模态学习过程中的表示平衡。

Result: 在AUC指标上，该方法的表现优于所有单模态基线、传统多模态模型以及当前最先进的平衡多模态模型。

Conclusion: 该模型有效解决了多模态学习中的不平衡问题，显著提升了青光眼检测性能，并可推广至糖尿病视网膜病变等其他疾病检测任务。

Abstract: Glaucoma is a group of eye diseases that damage the optic nerve, often caused by elevated intraocular pressure. It is a leading cause of irreversible vision loss and is typically developed slowly and painlessly, making it difficult to notice until significant damage has occurred. Therefore, early detection is crucial to prevent or slow the progression of vision loss. In recent years, deep learning based uni-modal models have improved the accuracy and efficiency of glaucoma detection, empowering doctors with tools for earlier diagnosis, better monitoring, and timely treatment. Building on this, multi-modal models have emerged, leveraging the strengths of different imaging modalities to learn richer and more robust representations, further enhancing glaucoma detection accuracy. However, multi-modal learning faces challenges such as imbalanced and under-optimized uni-modal representations due to joint learning objectives. To address this, we propose a balanced soft mixture-experts model with three experts and load balancing loss. The performance is measured by AUC, our proposed method surpasses the performance of all uni-modal baselines, conventional multi-modal models, and current stateof- the-art balanced multi-modal models. The proposed model can be generalized to other disease detections such as diabetic retinopathy.

</details>


### [31] [PanoLess: Environment Reconstruction from Partial Reflective Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25362)
*Ahitagni Das,Ashok Veeraraghavan,Vivek Boominathan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PanoLess框架，利用单侧反射面图像重建远处环境光照图。通过表面对齐的2D高斯泼溅和延迟着色恢复法线与反射线索，生成神经立方体贴图及可见性地图，实现从部分视图到完整环境的高保真、几何一致的光照估计，克服了传统方法对360度全覆盖的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有逆渲染和反射感知高斯泼溅方法通常要求完整的360度覆盖且在视图不完整时表现不佳的问题，旨在仅凭单侧反射面（如玻璃幕墙或反光物体）的图像即可重建周围环境，无需相机平移或获取全景图。

Method: 1. 构建基于高斯泼溅的框架PanoLess；2. 使用表面对齐的2D高斯泼溅结合延迟着色技术，从单侧反射图像中恢复准确的逐像素法线和反射线索；3. 将上述线索融合为环境的神经立方体贴图（Neural Cubemap）表示；4. 生成显式标记部分反射观测支持区域的可见性地图。

Result: 1. 实现了高保真且几何一致的环境重建；2. 在新的自定义合成基准和公开数据集上优于现有的反射感知基线方法；3. 证明了该方法在真实世界反射捕捉中的泛化能力；4. 能够从部分视图输入实现一致且符合物理规律的光照估计。

Conclusion: PanoLess成功证明了仅需单侧反射表面的部分视图即可准确重建周围环境的远距离光照分布，为受限视角下的环境感知提供了新的解决方案，具有显著的实用价值和泛化性能。

Abstract: Reflections from shiny objects and glass facades naturally extend the field of view of a camera, capturing the surrounding environment without the need to pan the camera or acquire a full panorama. We propose PanoLess, a Gaussian-splat-based framework that reconstructs the surrounding environment as a distant illumination map from images captured on only one side of a reflective surface. PanoLess leverages surface-aligned 2D Gaussian splats with deferred shading to recover accurate per-pixel normals and reflection cues, which are fused into a neural cubemap representation of the environment. In addition, PanoLess produces a visibility map that explicitly denotes which regions of the environment are supported by the partial reflective observations. Unlike existing inverse-rendering and reflection-aware Gaussian-splatting approaches, which typically require full 360-degree coverage and struggle under incomplete views, PanoLess enables consistent, physically grounded illumination estimation from partial-view input. We show that PanoLess achieves high-fidelity and geometrically consistent environment reconstruction, outperforming reflection-aware baselines on a new custom synthetic benchmark and publicly available datasets, and demonstrating generalization to real-world reflective captures.

</details>


### [32] [Leak-Free Cross-Validated Stacking with Per-Architecture Calibration for Sand-Boil Segmentation in Earthen Levees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25367)
*Padam Jung Thapa,Anav Katwal,Ayon Dey,Abdullah Bin Naeem,Steve Sloan,Kendall Niles,Md Tamjidul Hoque*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SandBoilNet框架，通过严格的数据隔离（合成图像过滤、跨验证集校准）解决沙沸点分割中因数据泄露导致的精度虚高问题。在测试集上IoU达0.707，优于原始方法；引入零标注成本的掩码条件合成技术提升性能至0.718。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有沙沸点检测研究存在两个导致准确率虚高的漏洞：一是在用于评分的同一组图像上调优集成权重；二是使用源自测试集照片的合成图像进行训练。这些做法违背了独立测试原则，需构建严谨的评估框架以获取真实性能指标。

Method: 1. 建立合成图像溯源机制，每张合成图标记其真实父源；2. 实施每折过滤策略，排除父源属于当前测试集的合成图像；3. 采用五折交叉验证训练五个编码器-解码器骨干网络，各用单一温度标量校准；4. 仅利用折外预测结果拟合像素级元学习者进行模型融合；5. 开发掩码条件合成路径，将条件掩码直接作为标签，实现零标注成本训练。

Result: 1. SandBoilNet在保持数据隔离的测试集上平均IoU为0.707，显著高于重新评估的原始方法（0.608）；2. 集成堆叠协议（0.681）未优于最佳单模型（0.694），归因于成员间高误差相关性（0.894）；3. 经标签保真度过滤的合成池使最佳模型IoU提升至0.718；4. 掩码条件合成成功生成带标签训练图像。

Conclusion: 通过消除数据泄露漏洞，SandBoilNet提供了更可靠的沙沸点分割性能基准。研究表明简单单模型在低多样性下优于复杂集成，而高质量合成数据与零标注合成技术可进一步提升分割精度，为稀缺标注场景下的深度学习应用提供方法论参考。

Abstract: Sand boils, points where water seeping beneath an earthen levee re-emerges at the surface, are early warnings of internal erosion, and deep segmentation networks are increasingly used to find them in inspection photographs. Annotated examples are scarce, and two common ways of working around that scarcity quietly inflate reported accuracy: tuning ensemble weights on the same images later used to score them, and training on synthetic images derived from the very photographs held out for testing. We present a sand-boil segmentation framework that closes both loopholes. Every synthetic image carries a pointer to its real parent, and a per-fold filter excludes any image whose parent is held out; five encoder-decoder backbones are trained under five-fold cross-validation, calibrated by one temperature scalar each, and combined by a per-pixel meta-learner fitted only on out-of-fold predictions. On the held-out test set the proposed Updated SandBoilNet reaches an intersection-over-union of 0.707 over three seeds, against 0.608 for the published original re-evaluated on the same split. Under the stacking protocol the calibrated stack reaches 0.681 against 0.694 for the strongest fold-averaged member, so it does not improve on the best single model; eight meta-learner families reproduce that outcome, which we trace to a mean pairwise error correlation of 0.894 among members. A synthetic pool filtered for label fidelity lifts the champion to 0.718 over three seeds against a 0.707 control. We also introduce a mask-conditioned synthesis route that makes the conditioning mask the label by construction, giving labelled training images at zero annotation cost.

</details>


### [33] [Hyperspectral Intrinsic Decomposition: Joint Recovery of Reflectance and Photometric Components for Non-Lambertian Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25371)
*Hao Ye,Zhan Shi,Chenglong Huang,Tao Lv,Mingjie Ji,Qiu Shen,Xun Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于改进二色反射模型（DRM）的统一逆范式，通过双尺度分解方案解决高光谱图像在非朗伯体假设下的盲分解问题，并发布了首个公开数据集CITE及数据生成器PISG。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高光谱本征分解（HID）研究多局限于朗伯或近朗伯假设，而现有的非朗伯方法依赖简化的镜面反射假设，无法处理复杂的真实世界镜面效应，且通常需要辅助输入或仅恢复部分组件，阻碍了完整且盲式的分解。

Method: 1. 重新审视二色反射模型（DRM），将四个耦合的反射率和光度分量的恢复重构为两个光谱-空间目标变量的估计；2. 提出双尺度分解方案：全局尺度利用光度不变描述符作为边缘先验以保留边界细节；局部尺度利用镜面引导注意力机制，重点细化受镜面效应主导及裁剪失真影响的区域。

Result: 在自建的首个非朗伯物体真实世界HID数据集CITE及其他高光谱图像上进行了广泛实验和消融研究，证明了该方法的有效性及在不同场景下的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该研究成功实现了复杂非朗伯条件下的高光谱图像完整盲分解，并通过发布CITE数据集和PISG生成器促进了未来相关研究的发展。

Abstract: Hyperspectral intrinsic decomposition (HID) aims to disentangle material-related spectral properties and photometric effects in hyperspectral images (HSIs), which is essential for understanding real-world imaging processes and benefits a variety of downstream applications. Most existing HID studies have been developed under Lambertian or near-Lambertian assumptions. The few prior non-Lambertian efforts rely on simplified specular assumptions insufficient to handle diverse real-world specularity, and typically require auxiliary inputs or recover only a subset of the coupled reflectance and photometric components, hindering complete and blind decomposition. In this paper, we revisit the dichromatic reflection model (DRM) and develop a unified inversion paradigm that reformulates the recovery of four coupled reflectance and photometric components as the estimation of two spectral--spatial target variables. Building on this reformulation, we propose a dual-scale decomposition scheme to handle non-Lambertian effects with distinct spatial characteristics. At the global scale, photometrically invariant descriptors serve as edge priors for high-fidelity intrinsic boundary preservation; at the local scale, specularity-guided attention directs refinement with emphasis on specularity-dominated regions, including those affected by clipping distortion.
